hyphens-auto">Conversation History</span></div></div></a></li></ul></div><script>(function () {
  try {
    if (window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone) return;
    window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone = true;

    var path = (window.location.pathname || '/').split('#')[0].split('?')[0];
    if (path.endsWith('/index')) path = path.slice(0, -6);
    else if (path === 'index') path = '';

    var candidates = [];
    if (path) candidates.push(path);
    if (path.startsWith('/')) candidates.push(path.slice(1));
    else candidates.push('/' + path);

    var item = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length && !item; i++) {
      var matches = document.querySelectorAll('[id="' + candidates[i].replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]');
      for (var j = 0; j < matches.length; j++) {
        if (matches[j].closest('#sidebar, #sidebar-content')) {
          item = matches[j];
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!item) return;

    var parent = item.closest('[data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"]');
    if (!parent) {
      parent = item.parentElement;
      while (parent) {
        var style = getComputedStyle(parent);
        if (style.overflowY === 'auto' || style.overflowY === 'scroll') break;
        parent = parent.parentElement;
      }
    }
    if (!parent) return;

    var parentRect = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var itemRect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

    var itemTopRelative = itemRect.top - parentRect.top + parent.scrollTop;
    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;
  } catch (e) {}

overflow-wrap-anywhere cursor-pointer" data-component-part="field-name"><span class="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">thinking.</span>clear_thinking</div><div class="inline items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium [&amp;_div]:inline [&amp;_div]:mr-2 [&amp;_div]:leading-5" data-component-part="field-meta"><div class="flex items-center gap-1 break-all rounded-md bg-stone-100/50 px-2 py-0.5 font-medium text-stone-600 dark:bg-white/5 dark:text-stone-200" data-component-part="field-info-pill"><span>boolean</span></div><div class="flex items-center gap-1 break-all rounded-md bg-stone-100/50 px-2 py-0.5 font-medium text-stone-600 dark:bg-white/5 dark:text-stone-200" data-component-part="field-info-pill"><span class="text-stone-400 dark:text-stone-500">default:</span><span>true</span></div></div></div></div></div><div class="mt-4"><div class="space-y-4 whitespace-normal prose prose-sm prose-gray dark:prose-invert overflow-wrap-anywhere [&amp;_*]:overflow-wrap-anywhere [&amp;_ul]:list-none [&amp;_ul&gt;li]:before:top-[0.75em] [&amp;_li_p.whitespace-pre-line]:!my-0 [&amp;_li.py-1]:!py-0 [&amp;_li.py-1]:!my-2"><p class="whitespace-pre-line">Default value is True. Controls whether to clear <code node="[object Object]"><span>reasoning_content</span></code> from previous conversation turns. View more in <a class="link" href="/guides/capabilities/thinking-mode">Thinking Mode</a>.</p>
<ul class="list-inside list-disc whitespace-normal" data-streamdown="unordered-list">
<li class="py-1 [&amp;&gt;p]:inline" data-streamdown="list-item"><code node="[object Object]"><span>true</span></code> (default): For this request, the system ignores/removes <code node="[object Object]"><span>reasoning_content</span></code> from prior turns, and only keeps non-reasoning context (e.g., user/assistant visible text, tool calls, and tool results). This is recommended for general chat or lightweight tasks to reduce context length and cost.</li>
<li class="py-1 [&amp;&gt;p]:inline" data-streamdown="list-item"><code node="[object Object]"><span>false</span></code>: Retains <code node="[object Object]"><span>reasoning_content</span></code> from prior turns and includes it in the context sent to the model. To enable Preserved Thinking, you must forward the full, unmodified, and correctly ordered historical <code node="[object Object]"><span>reasoning_content</span></code> in <code node="[object Object]"><span>messages</span></code>. Missing, truncated, rewritten, or reordered blocks may degrade performance or prevent the feature from taking effect.</li>
<li class="py-1 [&amp;&gt;p]:inline" data-streamdown="list-item">Notes: This parameter only affects cross-turn historical thinking blocks; it does not change whether the model generates/returns thinking in the current turn.</li>
</ul></div><div class="flex prose prose-sm prose-gray dark:prose-invert mt-6 gap-1.5"><span>Example<!-- -->:</span><div class="space-y-4 whitespace-normal prose prose-sm prose-gray dark:prose-invert
